Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The mechanics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 853 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 637 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_853(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 853. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^637(S, F) is trivial. QED.
